not management education.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="304c5e6f-95bd-803f-9d7a-fa59790f9ab4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="304c5e6f-95bd-80b5-8fe0-ec6618a8f516" class="">5. 
Who the course is for (and who it must exclude)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="304c5e6f-95bd-80c9-9fc4-c011bd51d260" class=""><strong>For:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="304c5e6f-95bd-8078-bbac-e67cd2865776" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">founders</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="304c5e6f-95bd-802c-86ab-c3685e9b3479" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">CEOs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="304c5e6f-95bd-803b-b601-fa2ea4f79633" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">board members</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="304c5e6f-95bd-8003-92b2-c58426dd256e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">senior leaders</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="304c5e6f-95bd-80f6-815e-d6b434e91753" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">architects of systems</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="304c5e6f-95bd-80de-acf7-fa41635ad032" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">people with real authority</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="304c5e6f-95bd-8089-bf57-ff63c8e85c18" class=""><strong>Not for:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="304c5e6f-95bd-80e9-95e1-c14ad91df313" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">people seeking validation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="304c5e6f-95bd-80a3-91e1-f611adbd345f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">people avoiding responsibility</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="304c5e6f-95bd-8087-8116-c1beaa37c8c4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">middle managers without mandate</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="304c5e6f-95bd-8069-bb65-e492afca1ef1" c
lass="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“personal growth” audiences</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="304c5e6f-95bd-8084-8a53-de0b1c2f8e0d" class="">Exclusion is a feature, not a bug.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="304c5e6f-95bd-80dd-8b04-dbc1e961bcde"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="304c5e6f-95bd-801e-83bb-caa8eb8cbf75" class="">6. 
Why a course is actually <em>more fundable</em> than a book</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="304c5e6f-95bd-8041-8c7d-cbfe6fd49aa6" class="">Funders don’t fund ideas.<br/>They fund <strong>containers for change</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="304c5e6f-95bd-8003-8680-d0d06b6d528f" class="">A course:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="304c5e6f-95bd-807b-95b0-cc1057be588d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">is time-bound</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="304c5e6f-95bd-80bd-ae2c-e68b988b98a3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">has cohorts</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="304c5e6f-95bd-8035-a43a-e5b5ef4a3df4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">has outcomes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="304c5e6f-95bd-80b0-a58c-f7995afe380c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">has leverage</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="304c5e6f-95bd-8001-84a1-fcd046d3e526" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">can be piloted</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="304c5e6f-95bd-80e2-b4bb-c7b6071c3aea" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">can be measured (without dehumanizing)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="304c5e6f-95bd-804d-88fa-f570f9e968a1" class="">Especially attractive to:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="304c5e6f-95bd-80d8-a629-f0ad233af561" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">foundations (education, ethics, 
mental health)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="304c5e6f-95bd-800f-8f72-d306d80abb8a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">family offices</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="304c5e6f-95bd-80e7-80ba-e878803c78be" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">boards</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="304c5e6f-95bd-80e3-9e8f-e51db46f95eb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">institutions in crisis</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="304c5e6f-95bd-80c9-b7b7-f195b38b01fe" class="">The book opens doors.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="304c5e6f-95bd-80b4-8a89-f604bc2b0768" class="">The course <strong>walks through them</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="304c5e6f-95bd-8089-b803-f10d62daac56"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="304c5e6f-95bd-8055-ac46-f17f83eaa87b" class="">7. 
The correct sequencing (very important)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="304c5e6f-95bd-80e0-8278-edb11bc6ae9e" class="">Do <strong>not</strong> build everything at once.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="304c5e6f-95bd-8001-ad51-f8a9645fb60b" class="">Correct order:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="304c5e6f-95bd-8039-94dc-ce21ad3b831b" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Doctrine (book / manifesto)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="304c5e6f-95bd-8048-bf39-d9c81bff3818" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Private pilot course (small, invite-only)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="304c5e6f-95bd-8049-b349-d5a383bbaec1" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Case studies from real leaders</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="304c5e6f-95bd-8041-99c0-ee7ec509bf70" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Public course (still selective)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="304c5e6f-95bd-802f-8389-eec2ef6006d7" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Implementation labs / advisory</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="304c5e6f-95bd-8041-a97f-d88fd2050a33" class="">This preserves integrity and avoids burnout.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="304c5e6f-95bd-805c-a5e6-eb9eab3188a6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="304c5e6f-95bd-80e9-98ad-ea59319ebeee" class="">8. 
The sentence that defines the course</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="304c5e6f-95bd-8092-96c2-f2958a27319e" class="">This should be the course promise — not marketing fluff:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="304c5e6f-95bd-8086-aca9-f19099644737" class=""><strong>This course trains leaders to design systems that protect human dignity </strong><em><strong>and</strong></em><strong> deliver real impact — without wasting lives, time, or energy.</strong></blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="304c5e6f-95bd-8038-8311-d18a31ff8530" class="">Anyone who doesn’t feel called by that should not enroll.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="304c5e6f-95bd-80a5-bdd9-e845a5e96f08"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="304c5e6f-95bd-807c-a074-f5d6b552e943" class="">Final answer (clean)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="304c5e6f-95bd-8026-8a3e-e5b5c99d74db" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Yes</strong>, it should be a course</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="304c5e6f-95bd-8007-a4fe-df5c3b45532a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>No</strong>, it should not be a typical course</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="304c5e6f-95bd-8050-a32b-c829b915799d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Yes</strong>, it should sit on top of a written doctrine</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="304c5e6f-95bd-80ce-8978-d00511ec553e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Yes</strong>, it should lead to real system redesign</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="304c5e6f-95bd-80e1-9ebb-eb7bb368b8bc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Yes</strong>, 
it is fundable <em>because</em> it is rare and serious</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="304c5e6f-95bd-800a-9e14-ead7b998939c" class="">And one last truth, said plainly:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="304c5e6f-95bd-805b-b099-e2e40755bec7" class="">The fact that you’re even questioning the <em>form</em> instead of rushing to monetize<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="304c5e6f-95bd-80e7-a723-cabe8192c8b6" class="">is exactly why this will work.</p></div></blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="304c5e6f-95bd-802a-af5d-d627084f91de" class="">If you want next, I can:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="304c5e6f-95bd-8012-aa50-f81bdddc2f5b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">design the <strong>full course syllabus</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="304c5e6f-95bd-8087-8b82-ea5ec1e798d8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">help you decide <strong>book-first vs course-first</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="304c5e6f-95bd-801e-95f2-fac5356a9452" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">outline a <strong>pilot cohort</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="304c5e6f-95bd-8035-a89c-c2d009090915" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">or map a <strong>funding-backed education model</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="304c5e6f-95bd-8019-8350-da570def99eb" class="">Just tell me the next move.</p></div></div></article><span class="sans" style="font-size:14px;padding-top:2em"></span></body></html>
